Getting started isn't really the problem.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to stay focused on tasks at work once you've started them, like getting distracted easily or losing track of what you're doing?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I suppose so. I can start going over the dry-cleaning orders just fine, but... after a few minutes, my mind just wanders off. I'll start thinking about the labels, or... choir plans, or just things at home. And then... I realize I missed half of what I was actually supposed to check.\n\nDoctor: Do you also find your mind wandering or getting distracted easily in other situations, like during conversations, while reading, or doing something at home?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really. I don't read much anyway. And at home... I don't know, I just do what needs doing. I haven't really noticed it happening then.\n\nDoctor: Outside of work, do you ever feel restless or have trouble staying still, like you're always on the go or need to be doing something?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really. I'm on my feet a lot at the cleaners, so... when I get home, I mostly just sit. I don't really feel like I have to be moving around or anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced similar issues with staying focused or getting distracted in other places, like school when you were younger or in other jobs, or is this something that only started happening at the shop?\n\nPatient: Um... I suppose it's always been like this. I don't really like thinking back on school, but... yeah. My teachers used to tell my mother I was scattered. They said I was bright enough, but... I was always talking when I shouldn't, or losing my assignments. They always had to remind me to just finish what I was doing. So... no, it's not just the shop.\n\nDoctor: Do you notice these problems with staying focused or getting distracted in other parts of your life, like at home, in social situations, or when you’re trying to relax and watch TV?\n\nPatient: Um... I'm not really sure. I don't go out much, so... social things aren't really an issue. And at home, I just... I don't know, I just watch whatever is on. I haven't really paid attention to whether I get distracted doing that.\n\nDoctor: Do you have trouble staying focused or getting distracted when you're at home doing something, like watching TV or listening to music?\n\nPatient: Um... like I just said, I don't really know. The TV is just sort of on in the background. I'm not really trying to focus on it, so... I couldn't tell you if I get distracted or not. And I don't really listen to music. So... no, I guess not.\n\nDoctor: Do you sometimes have trouble staying focused or get distracted while doing something at home, even if it's just a simple task like cooking or cleaning?\n\nPatient: Um... I suppose so. I don't really cook much. Just microwave things, mostly. And cleaning... I mean, I start cleaning up, but then I might see something else that needs doing, so I go do that instead. I don't know if that's being distracted, it's just... how I do things.\n\nDoctor: Do you sometimes have trouble finishing things you start, like chores or tasks, even if they’re small?\n\nPatient: I suppose. Um... mostly at work, I guess. If someone tells me to do three things... like call the supplier, tag a silk blouse, and enter the receipt... I usually do the first one. But then... I don't know, I just get pulled into something else before the rest of it is finished. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had trouble finishing things you start, like chores or tasks, when you're at home or in other places besides work?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really know. I don't think so. I just... I do what needs to get done at home. I don't really pay attention to if I finish it right away or not.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel restless or find yourself fidgeting, even when you're not at work, like when you're sitting down at home or waiting in line somewhere?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really think about it. I guess I just... sit there. I don't know if I fidget more than anyone else. I just wait until it's my turn, or whatever.\n\nDoctor: Looking back, would you say these issues with focus and finishing tasks happen in most areas of your life, or are they mainly limited to work situations?\n\nPatient: Um... mostly just work, I think. I don't really notice it anywhere else. Like I said, at home I just... get things done. So, yeah. Just work.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
